# Skills · I · part 61

850 entries, ion-mobility-feature-classification to ios-hig. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ion-mobility-feature-classification**: Use when you have raw or processed TWIM-MS data (arrival time and m/z pairs) from multiple lipid, protein, or metabolite classes and need to classify features by biomolecular type before—or instead of—performing feature identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/ion-mobility-feature-classification))
- **ion-mobility-feature-engineering**: Use when you have a set of metabolite structures (or their molecular descriptors) and need to construct training or target feature matrices for CCS prediction. Specifically, use it when you are preparing data to fit or apply a machine learning model (e.g., Sklearn v1.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-feature-engineering) · 2 copies)
- **ion-mobility-filtering-and-tolerance-tuning**: Use when your raw MSI dataset is acquired on an ion-mobility-enabled instrument (e.g., Bruker .baf, .tsf, or .tdf formats) and your analyte of interest has a known or experimentally determined ion mobility value (1/K0 or drift time μs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-filtering-and-tolerance-tuning) · 2 copies)
- **ion-mobility-heatmap-visualization**: Use when you have raw LC-IMS-MS data (Agilent, Thermo, Bruker, or mzML format) and need to visualize and export the spatial distribution of a specific ion species (or ion family) across both ion mobility and retention time dimensions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-heatmap-visualization) · 3 copies)
- **ion-mobility-heatmap-visualization**: Use when raw multidimensional MS data (e.g., Agilent .d or SLIM-MS LC-IM-MS runs) has been converted to .mza and you need to visualize mobility distributions or benchmark non-vendor rendering speed of an ion mobility heatmap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-heatmap-visualization))
